The Witness Protection Act has been under practice for about five years. In response to a question motion, 38 people have been reportedly "witnessed" and 117 have been "protected as a witness" by the beginning of 2012, 43 of whom have been "changed information and relevant documents," and 39 others have been reportedly "to be living in another residential settlement." Witnesses who are likely to influence judgment need to be reliable and truly truthful. And for that, in the courts, "I swear on my honor and my conscience that I told you what I knew." under oath. Reliability is natural, which must also apply to confidential witnesses under the Witness Protection Act. Recently, the most common use of secret witness application appears to be cases in Istanbul Silivri. Secret witnesses are charging the defendants with serious charges. However, the testimony that many confidential witnesses made at one trial is reflected in the public opinion that several secret witnesses had changed at another trial, that there were many of them in the same files. In addition, many of the confidential witnesses have been identified. It turns out that many of these people have been convicted of many crimes, including extortion, murder, rape, and rape, which they themselves are criminal machines. In addition, it appears that even those convicted of these crimes, even those who have participated in the attacks that killed many of our citizens and security officers, who have led the separatist terrorist organization, have been made secret witnesses, who have filed serious charges against public officials who have served in the fight against terrorism. There are cases in which secret witness practice has negatively affected the defendant's right to defend, where the defendants are trying to refute claims and accusations that they do not know who said it for what purpose, and they are trying to prove that they are not true. In fact, in the trial, the guilt of the accused must be presented with concrete evidence and testimony based on evidence by reliable witnesses. The secret witness application reveals the opposite. A fair trial is a trial in which the right to defend is guaranteed. Examples of the secret witness application create hesitations about it. In this scope, a parliamentary examination of the results of the Witness Protection Act is necessary to assess the implementation of the implementation of an effective system, to examine the positive, negative consequences and effects of a fair trial.
